- **lazy-wiki.audit**: Run when the operator asks to check the wiki's health, or when the wiki misbehaves — `/lazy-wiki.query` misses covered material or returns entries that no longer exist, See-also links point at moved nodes, the topic index disagrees with the disk, or documents and the terms dictionary use different words for one concept. Read-only: it reports and leaves the repairs to the operator. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-wiki/skills/lazy-wiki.audit) · ★1)
- **lazy-wiki.configure**: Use when the user wants to add a wiki scope or change what one covers — paths, axes, exclusions, topics index — or to set up domain specs (`domains`), a foreign-repo mirror (`mirror`), a terms dictionary (`terms`), the structure map (`structure`), or the vault-wide wiki keys (`vault`). Defines coverage only; bootstrapping the plugin is `/lazy-wiki.install`. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-wiki/skills/lazy-wiki.configure) · ★1)
- **lazy-wiki.domain-sync**: Use when the domain-spec tree needs regenerating right now — this checkout runs no runtime daemon, domain markers or the dictionary just changed and the operator wants the docs current, or a `/lazy-python.knowledge-sweep` backfill has landed. An optional group key limits the run to one group; reading the tree is `/lazy-wiki.domains`. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-wiki/skills/lazy-wiki.domain-sync) · ★1)
- **lazy-wiki.domains**: Use when a question needs domain knowledge from code's `Domain(…)` markers — defining a term, looking up a mechanic or formula, or surveying a group of related concepts — and `wiki.domains` is configured. A research skill: it hands back a bounded slice of the domain-spec tree, never the whole tree; regenerating the tree is `/lazy-wiki.domain-sync`. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-wiki/skills/lazy-wiki.domains) · ★1)
- **lazy-wiki.install**: Run when the operator asks to set up the wiki in a repo, after a lazycortex-wiki update, or when wiki skills fail because the `lazy-wiki.navigation` rule, a `wiki` / `structure` / `terms` settings section, or a wiki expert is missing. Bootstrap only — what the wiki, the terms dictionary or the structure map covers is `/lazy-wiki.configure`. - **lazy-wiki.query**: Use when a question needs material the wiki curates — 'why is it built this way', 'where is X described', 'what relates to Y', or any request whose answer lives in files a wiki scope covers. - **lazy-wiki.relink**: Use when a wiki scope's nodes need classifying and See-also linking right now — this checkout runs no runtime daemon, or the operator wants to force a relink instead of waiting for the `lazy-wiki.scan` / `lazy-wiki.relink-weekly` routines. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-wiki/skills/lazy-wiki.relink) · ★1)
- **lazy-wiki.structure**: Use when an agent doing research (an architect deciding where a new file belongs, any expert asking 'where does X live in this repo') needs the project's structure map, or when the operator asks to resync `docs/structure.md` after files moved. `query [&lt;path&gt;]` returns just the slice under `&lt;path&gt;`; `rebuild` rewrites the map.
